[Overview](index.md) · [Agent Context](agent-context.md) · [Extracted Data](details.md) · [Network](network.md) · [RBAC](rbac.md) · [Security](security.md) · [Cache](cache.md) · [Dataflow](dataflow.md)

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- kuberay is a Kubernetes component.
- deploying 4 workload(s) (kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator) with 4 container(s).
- watching 13 resource type(s).
- exposing 2 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8080.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 3000.
- with 6 webhook(s) (4 validating, 2 mutating).
- connecting to external services\: azure-blob, gcs, grpc, requests, urllib.
- runtime dependencies\: MinIO/S3, Redis, S3, kuberay-apiserver-service.
- with 998 operator config constants (20 image references, 150 env vars).
- exposing 8 Prometheus metrics.
- Python k8s client with 30 API call(s) and 1 CRD kind reference(s).
- integrates with Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization.
- references components\: vllm (import).
